sofzh_logistics_seed_transport = {
 remove_ideas = no_supply_cons
 if = {
  limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = sofzh_logistics_stock_seeded_299 } }
  set_technology = { basic_train = 1 motorised_infantry = 1 popup = no }
  add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = 4 }
  add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = 20 }
  every_owned_state = {
   limit = { sofzh_logistics_french_state = yes }
   PREV = {
    add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = 2 }
    add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = 4 }
   }
  }
  set_country_flag = sofzh_logistics_stock_seeded_299
 }
}
